8a1(dza): @#sku gsum gyi tha ma sprul pa'i sku la/gsung gi tha ma ni tshig gsung bsgrags/thu gsum gyis tha ma ni 'jig rten mkhyen pa'i thugs dgongs nas/'gro ba 'dul ba'i don du sku mdog 'thing ka/zhal

8a3(dza): du mas brgyan te/nyid kyis nyid la che ba rngom pa'i tshig 'di smra so//da ni khro bo rnam par rgyal/chos ku brdal ba chen pos ni/skye ba med pa'i chos dbyingasu//mtshan ma med pa'i chos rnams bshad/chos kyi dbyings kyi yis//long skyong rdzog sku mtshan dang ngo pe byad
8a4(dza): kyis/seng+ge glang chen khri stengs nas/dus gsum sangs rgyas rmad la/yod du med pa'i chos kyang bstan/me longs lta bu'i yi so//sprul pas 'gro don byed pa'i skus/'jig rten pal ba'i sna rnams//dngos po mtshan ma'i chos bstan pas/khya ba nan
8a5(dza): tan yai so//tshar gsum can gyis rnge'u chung gis/lcang lo can gyi pho brang bcom/snang mdzad chen po a la la/gnod sbyin chen po a la la/chos kyi dbyings nyid a la la/sang+yas bden tshig a la la/tshar gsum can gyis rnge'u chung gis/'jig/
8a6(dza): rten phal pa'i sar brdungs pas//mtshan ma dngos po'i chos rnams las/yod med chos las spyod par shog/tshar gsum can gyi rde'u chung gis/'og min gnas kyi sar bsdus pas/yod myed chos la spyod pa rnams//rang bzhin med pa'i don rtogs
8a7(dza): shog/de skad ces smras pas/stong gsum gyis stong chen po'i 'jig rten khams tha~Mng /de ltar ma gyur gcig kyang med/dper na dem tshig mchog rab yin/spang bar bya ba ma yin te/gtso bo rab tu sdug pi li/drang du smra ba gang byed pa/bsgrigs ni/
8b1(dza): tshig gis brgyan nas//sri zhu sems kyi gdung ba la/yis tshogs kyis rab bskor ba/mnyam pa'i dbyings nyid yis te/gnyis ka nges par mnyam na ni/stong gsum dbang por gdon mi za//'jig rten khams na rgyal bzhin/dus gsum kun gyis gnas
8b2(dza): dang rten//dper na rang gi gnas khung du/rang gi dri chen maN+Da la bya/rang gi gur gum chag chag gdab/spos dang du ru ka ru dang /dza ti pa la mchog tu bsngags/me tog phreng bas kun tu brgyan/de lta bu yi gnas mchog tu/gzhan zhig nges par bsgral na ni/rdo srin
8b3(dza): po'i ting 'dzin sgom/ragra'i migs lta bar bya/kha nas sngags kyis tshig bzlos shig/pha rol rnam shes hUM gis drang /snying khor mngon sum dmigs bya na/pha rol mngon sum 'chi bar 'gyur/mgo dang snying dang rkang lag dang /ya min ta ka'i khor
8b4(dza): la 'bul/gzhan yang lhag ma ci yod pa/he ru ka la yon du dbul/gnas lugs 'phrul gyi dgongs pa'o//de lta bu'i dus ngag tu/lag cha phur pa mchog yin te/bzhi pa'i sor bdun zlum por bya/rgyas pa'i sor brgyad zur yang brgyad/dbang
8b5(dza): la zla gam nges par bzang /drag po'i sor drug zur gsum pa'o//kun gyi rtse mor hUM bzhag ste/de su ha ha ru la sgom/rgya mdud thog tu oM bas mo//de lta bu'i dus ngag tu/pho nya 'od zer dung kha 'phro//shar gyi phyogs nas mngag pa ni/mgo dgu
8b6(dza): pa la rkang gcig po//lus nag po la 'od dpung gnag/ral pa gnag cing mche ba gnag/mig kyang gnag tsang lag pa gnag/mgyogs shing rlung gis bskyong du med/'di dag dpal gyi pho nya ste/zhum pa med cing stobs chen 'joms/gapra
8b7(dza): ba gsum ni sta re 'phyar/g.yon pa gsum ni lcags kyu 'dzin/yan lag sbrul nag mchog gis brgyan/g.yon gyis lcags kyus snying nas drongs/g.yas kyi star nges par bcom/'di ni sgo bsrungs mchog yin te/pho nya rgyun du mngag par/
9a1(dza): bya bgegs rnams nges par phung bar byed/rkang gcig de yi pho nya ni/kha dog 'bar ba ci 'dra na/gcig la gcig gis rab tu 'khril/yang na lag pa sbrel te 'gro//mig kyang med cing cang mi smra/long ba kun
9a2(dza): gyis mchog yin te/rta babs kun gyi sgor bsgoms te/lan 'ga' drag tu mngag pa'i tshe/gnyis ka song nas der phyin te/gang la dmigs pa len par byed/sus kyang bzlog par mi nuso//lho yis phyogs nas bkye ba ni/mgo brgyad
9a3(dza): po la lag brgyad po//rkang pa brgyad la mig kyang med/g.yas pa bzhi ni rdo rje 'dzin/g.yon pa bzhi ni phur pa 'dzin/ha ha ru lu'i sgra sgrogs shing /gshin rje'i gnas rnams nges par 'joms/mgo rgyad de'i pho nya ni/hUM dang phaT kyi sgra
9a4(dza): sgrogs shing /bskal pa'i 'brug sgra chen po bzhin/kun kyang mgyogs pa rlung dang mtshungs//ha ha ru lu'i sgra sgrogs shing /gang la dmigs pa len par byed/nub kyi phyogs nas dgye ba ni/mgo drug lag drug rkang drug po//g.yas/
9a5(dza): pa gsum ni phur pa 'di la/g.yon pa gsum ni phur pa 'dril/g.yon pa gsum ni gtor ma gtong /mgyogs ni rlung gi shugs dang 'dra/'di ni dpal gyi pho nya yin/ha ha ru lu'i sgra sgrogs shing /rang gi rlung phyogs song ba'i tshe//khro
9a6(dza): bo kun la nges par 'chi/rkang drug de'i pho nya ni/lcags kyi spyang mo rkang gsum ni/gang nas gang du 'gro ba'i tshe/gzugs la brdog pa rdeg cing 'gro/rang gi rkang pa phur pa yin/lcags kyis phur ba rab tu drag/'od zer dung kas reg
9a7(dza): pa ni tshangs pa'i gnas kyang 'jigs byed na/gzugs can sgra khyod smos ci dgos/byang gi phyogs nas dge ba ni/mgo gsum lag drug rkang gsum po//lag pa drug ni rgyang drug 'joms//mi shi zas zhing bdud rnams sgrol//gnod/
9b1(dza): sbyin gnas rnams 'joms par byed/gang gsum ngor po ni/byang gi phyogs nas rgyug pa yi/lcags kyi kyi nang gsum la/sngags kyis zhus la nyi ma'i mig/spyan ljags kyi bsgril pa bzhin/pang ni rlung gis bsrung du med/
9b2(dza): skad ni gnam gyi 'brug pas che/rab tu mi bsrun 'di dag ni/phyag na rdo'i mthu yang 'phrog/rkang gcig gyen la bsgrengs pa yis/ya bdud chen po'i snying las 'dren/rkang gcig phur du byung ba ni/ma bdud chen po'i snying las 'dren/rkang
9b3(dza): gcig thang dkar byung bas ni/dgra bo ma lus snying las 'dren/'byung po ma lus nya la thag cod/sku ni lcags theg bsgril ba 'dra//tshems ni spu dri gshegs pa 'dra/maM sa za zhing ri klungs rgyug/'jig rten khams kun zab mo//
9b4(dza): gnod sbyin kun gyi steng du rgyug/glu snying nad khrol 'byin zhing za khar snang khamasu khros pa'i tshe/nyi zla kun kyang zang la rlog/bza' skar kun kyang dum bur gcod/shin tu khros nas sbas pa'i tshe/bdud kyi snying khrag dron mo 'thung /rdul tsa
9b5(dza): yang med par mdzod/ku kur tsi kha lo kha ces brjod cing spo //de nas 'di rnams bstan pa'i phyir/dbang chen nyid kyis bka' stsal pa/sgrub pa'i cho ga rdzogs pa'i tshe/bar snang rdo sems dpa' yis/cha lugs ci 'dra 'khor dang bcas/
9b6(dza): sems kyis mnyam par dgod par bya//bdag phra men ma'i 'khor mang po dang /gnod sbyin gyi 'khor mang po rol par 'dod na/bdag dbang chen gyi tingange 'dzin du zhugs te/sku mdog dkar por bsgyur te/glu dang bros brten par bya'o//bdag
9b7(dza): 'khor mang po dang mnyam par 'jug pa'i dus na/sku mdog dmar por bgyur//lam du zhugs 'gro ba'i dus na/sku mdog ljang khur bsgyur te nye sa zhes bya ste rgod cing 'gro'o//gang du phyin pa'i gnas hU~M zhes byas la 'du'o//'on tang
10a1(dza): ma mothong dbang du 'du'o/sa pa'i chen a zhes byas la cog tsog por lud nam nas gnas par bya'o//dag ny-id gser ba co mo'i kha dog bzhin 'tshor bar bsgyur te/kun tu bzad ma namakha' las langasanas mdzan
10a2(dza): /du 'dzum pa mdzad pa dang /snyan par smra ba dang /'dod pa sbyor ba'i long spyod kyis rtse mo yang byas par bas mo//de ltar bsams pa'i yon tan ni/sa'i 'og tu mda' 'phangs pas/gnas kyi steng gi lha bran 'chi/spyod lam grub pa 'di ltar//mtshan
10a3(dza): thogs kun gyis rgyal po ste/ral gri 'dzin pa chen por 'gyur//'jam dpal 'khor gyis phyag byas pa'o//ces smras so//de lta bu yi dus dag tu/gnod sbyin phra men 'khor bcas kyis/bdag la rab tu brtse 'gyur na/phrags brtul zhugs
10a4(dza): chen po yis/sa yis 'og tu mngon sum bya/lus kyi hUM zhes brjod par bya/maM sa dag kyang mang du bza'/na mkha' la ni sems dpa' bsam/bgrang phreng klad la bskor bar bya/de ltar byas na bdag rgyal zhing /nad gtong mi ni 'ching bar
10a5(dza): 'gyur te de lta bu'i dus dag tu/gnod sbyin rnams las 'khor 'dod na/de'i gzugs ni khrag gis bri/dur khrod chen por song nas ni/snying khar phur pas mnan nas su/dgugs pa'i brtul zhugs hUM zhes bzlas//ma 'ongs par du bsdud na
10a6(dza): ni/zhag bdun gyis ni zhugs par 'gyur/gnod sbyin rnams la ci dgar spyod//bdag gis bran dang 'dra bar 'ong /phra men rnams la 'khol 'dod na/phra men gzugs ni khrag gis bri/yan lag phur bAs mnan nasu//snying khar rdeg
10a7(dza): bas mnan byas te/dgug pa'i brgyal zhugs sku nas bzlas/zhag bdun gyis ni nges par 'ong /phra men las la ci dgar 'khol/khong gis lan na khong dag 'chi/bdag gis lan na bdag nyid nyams/gnyis gtso las la 'a khol 'din/
10b1(dza): de'i gzugs ni khrag gis gri bur bu skyong dang kha rug dag dgag pa'i brtul zhugs cu zhes bzlas/zhag bdun gyis ni dpung du 'dun byal nardo rje dga' bar 'gyur/ma mo rnams las la 'khol 'dod na/bung med bu tshod khrag gis bri/skyi
10b2(dza): rdog pas nan nas ni/phur bu dril zhing bsgom par bya/zhag bdun gyis ni nges par rdung //ci bgyi gsung zhes zer te 'ong /de nas bdag gis spyad par bya/bud med rnams la ci dkur 'ong /bgegs rnams las la 'khol 'dod na/so so'i
10b3(dza): gzugs dag khrags bris/phur bu'i rtse mor ming bri zhing /gong du hUM gcig bzhag nas ni/tshon skud rnam lnga'i skud pas gdags/lan gsum bskor te gdab byas na/bdag gis dbang du 'du bar 'gyur/ji ltar bsgo ba nyan par byed/
10b4(dza): 'gro nyal 'dug sdod ci byed kyang /'khor dang bcas pa'i bsnyen pa 'bul/nang par mal nas langs nas kyang /yi dam gnas pa'i sa phyogs//gom gsum 'phags nas dran par bya//gshang gci byed pa'i sa dag tu/dri chen
10b5(dza): lag tu blangs nas kyang /phyogs bcu kun tu kha bltas te/sus ma mthong bar kham gsum za/dri chu khyor bar blugs nas ni/sus ma mthong bar hu ba gsum btang /shin tu dben pa'i sa phyogasu//dri zhim mdun du bzhag nas ni/yan lag
10b6(dza): bzhi'i sa la gzug/phag skad theng gsum ngur bar bya/ru lu ru lu tshal pa bzlas/gang gis phyogasu kha bltas pas/gnod sbyin dbang du bsdu ba'i phyir/dri chu mdun du bzhag nas ni//khyi skad zang gsum ngus nas ni/ru lu ru lu kha
10b7(dza): loyong kha ku ku ra tsa kha long kha//nyin dang mtshan du thun phrangadu/rig ma 'chol ba sbyar bar bya//skye bos mthong bar gyur na nyams//sus ma mthong na dngos grub yino//byang chub sems ni lce la bzhag/dus dang rnam pa med par ni//sbyor ba kho na wa
11a1(dza): su bzang /srab su bab pa'i dus su'o//dur khrod gnas thug par bya//shing dang ban+d+ha mang du bsags/rang gi dben sa gang yin pa/skye bo rnams las thag bsrings te srong dang tho rangs nam gyi grang //dra da nga kyi byed
11a2(dza): /dgongs ka'i dus//dbyangs dang skad drug shes par bya/dus drug kun tu rnge'u chung brdung /srong 'khor gsum po/brdungs te/nub kyi phyogasu kha bltas te/rta skad 'tsher ba'i gru skad blang //ru lu'i bsnyen pa 'bum bzlas shing /snum chen bsum
11a3(dza): gyi spos me btang /nam gyi gung la mi nyal zhing /phrin las gong ma bzhin du bya//byang gi phyogasu kha bltas te/glu dbyangs khyi skad thang gsum gzugs/tho rangs langs nas phyag rgyar gsal/byang shar 'tsham su kha bltas la/
11a4(dza): dbyangs ni shin tu gdung par bya//de yang bsnyen pa gong ma bzhin//dus la bab pa'i drod mchod la/phyi nang drag tu 'tshams bcad//rde'u chung dir dir lan gsum brdungs/sha khrag gtor ma rgyun mi bcang /glu bro shar gyi phyogasu dbul//
11a5(dza): nyim phyed kyi dus su ni/lho'i phyogasu kha bltas te/grul bum skad du ku co bya/phrin las dgongs pa gong ma bzhin/dgongs mchod dus la bab pa'i tshe/grib so bzlog pa'i cho ga bya/ha tsang skad ni mi che bar/der yang rde'u chung dir
11a6(dza): dir brdung da dbyangs ni yid tsam na brdung bar byud der yang dus drug kun tu yang /gsang ba'i mchod pa rgyun mi bcad/mi'i bu mo bsten par bya//lan 'ga' de dang ma ldan na/ku ku rgyu dang rgya la stsogs/ba dang bud med gang yang rung
11a7(dza): shin tu dkon nas med pa'i tshe/byes dang 'gron po byed pa na//lus las sprul pa'i lha mo yis/sbyor po'i dam tshig mi bca'o//de lta r+yan dngos grub thog rgyan dang rgyan med rtag par ni/sman ni lus la bsrel bar bya//
11b1(dza): sgrub pa chen sgo bzhi rnababs mu khyud bcas/drag po'i khro bo khyab la sogs/tshangs pa'i gdung chen bshag tu gtams/yang na tshangs pa'i gzhal yas ni/brgya byin lha'i dbang po bsgral//khyab 'jug chen po 'khor dang bcas//
11b2(dza): thog mchog a nu yo ga yi//gzhung las byung ba bzhin du bsgom//yang na tshangs pa'i gzhal yas ni/theg rgyal a ti yo ga'i gzhung /khyab dang gnas dang 'byung la sogs//rnam pa gsum po gzhal yas khang /snod bcud 'byung
11b3(dza): ba'i yum du gsal//ting 'dzin gsal ba skad cig la/bskyed nas gsang yum bzhi'i mkha'//b+ha ga dgyes pa'i nang du sgom//de'i dkyil du bdag po dmigs/de la rang bzhin med pa ste/nam mkha' lta bur rab rtogs na/gzhal
11b4(dza): yas khang zhes mkhas pas gsungs/dbang chen bsdus pa'i tan tre las/gzhal yas khang tshangs pa'i le'u ste brgyad pa'o///tshe la dbang thob rigs 'dzin rnal 'byor gyis/ting 'dzin gtan la dbab pa'i
11b5(dza): bAr dbang chen bsdus pa'i tan+da las/sgom khog rtsa ba'i le'u 'di gsungs//sbrum khog rtsa ba'i le'u 'di la yang le'u rnam pa bzhi ste/gang dang gang zhe na/sgrom khog rna ba'i le'u dang /gsang ba tshe'i le'u dang /sgrol ba thugs rje
11b6(dza): le'u dang /mdzad pa 'phrin las kyi le'u dang bzhi'o//dang por sgom khog rtsa ba'i le'u 'byung ste/'di skad ceso//rnam par mi rtog yis skye med cing /gzung 'dzin rtog pa'i mtha' gnyis ma chags dbyings//thod spros bral mnyam
11b7(dza): nyid ji bzhin dang /rnam rtag brjod med smra bsam yul las 'das/oM mahA shun+ya ta badz+ra sa b+hA wa ad ma ko&haM/ma ltar byams pas snying rje yongs la khyab/mkha' ltar go 'byed tshe 'phrul 'byung ba'i yum/kun la khya/
12a1(dza): @bas yas gsal ba'i ngang /rin chen gter ldan che ba'i khyad par ston//oM d+harma d+ha tu sa b+ha wa Ad ma ko&haM/yaM ge hUM ni sems kyi rang bzhin te/chags pa'i skyon bral rin chen sna sna'i mdog/'thung /
12a2(dza): ba dang lnga dang yum lnga yis lnga/gdul bya'i byin las rnam pa lnga ru sbyor/oM badz+ra tsit+ta hUM zhabs bskyed mthing ka'i sgra bsdu las/gsal ba'o go 'byed nam 'a 'gyur/dus gsu 'gyur ba med pas kun tu bzang /gtas ni 'byung ba
12a3(dza): kun la khyeg rna ru ljang ku'i sgro bsdu las/rgya gri rlung gi dkyi lor shar bar gsal/bde bskyed byed yum chen dam tshig sbrul/gtos ni na mkha'i mtha' dang mnyam/nga nyid dmar po'i 'phro 'du las/rak+Sha khrag gi rgya mtshor 'khyol//zang
12a4(dza): skyed pa'i yum chen mA ma ki/gtos ni na mkha'i mtha' dang mnyam/thig le sngo skya'i 'phro 'o las/keng rus chen po ri rab ste/thang brten pa'i yum chen sa d+yas spyan/gtos ni namakhwa'i mnyam/ha nyid ser po'i 'du 'phro 'du las/
12a5(dza): bskal pa'i me dpung chen por 'bar/'byung ba drod snyoms yum chen na bza' dkar/gtos ni na mkha'i mtha' dang mnyam/hUM mdzad hUM gis byin brlabs bsam mi khyag 'bru lngar spros pas yum lnga'i dkyi lor gsal/dbang
12a6(dza): chen yum du brtsegs pa'i byin brlabs kyis/yi ge hUM yang ma nyams na mkha'i dbyings na gsal/yi ge hUM gis byin brlabs kyis/dbang chen yum lnga'i mkhyil bu gzugs khams dbang du bsdu ba'i phyir/lha chen brgyad ni
12a7(dza): rmang du gting /'khod khams dbang du bsdu ba'i phyir/phyogs skyong bcu ni rtsig par brtsigs/sku bzhis dpal brkyang tshang ba'i phyir/rgyal chen bzhis ni ka bar bcug/gti mug la sogs yis bskyong pa'i phyir//klu chen brgyad ni
12b1(dza): gdudung byes/gzhal yas ma bsgribs yed mu gsal ba'i phyir/gza' chen brgyad ni gdon du bor/dus pa ni 'gyur ldog med par bya ba'i phyir/rgyus rnams ni phyogs nas mig chags byas/rgyal mtshan bzhi dang bla rer bcas
12b2(dza): nyasrul mchog phran za ra tsags/bde gshegs gzhal yas khang gi rang bzhi no//ai de ba ka pa la bruM/de ba ka pe la bru/de ba ka pa la bruM/oM rag sha kau pa la bruM/ragra ka pa la dzu/rAk+ka ka pa la bruM/au rgya tsa ka ba la
12b3(dza): bruM rgyaH ka pa la bruM/rgya tsa ka pa la bruM/nA ga ka pa la bruM/nA ga ka ba la bruM/sur+ya ka pa la dzu/sur+ya ka ba la bruM/sUr+ya ka pa la bruM/kro d+ha ka pa la bruM/kro d+ha ka pa la bruM/kro d+ha ka be la bruM/hA ha ru lu hUM phaT/ha ha ru lu hUM phaT/
12b4(dza): ru lu hUM phaT/ha ha ru lu hUM phaT/dza hUM ba~M ho//de lta bu'i dbus nyid du//dkyil 'khor gru bzhi maN+Dal/dbus ni zla gam e'i dkyil/bcu drug 'khor lo'i mtha' bskor zhing /hri las pad+ma nyi shu bzhi/zur bzhi rin chen khri rkang la/
12b5(dza): sprin ming nyi ma bdun gyi sa brgyan khor khyams gnyis dang skye bzhir ldan/bde gshegs bzhugs pa gnas kyi dbyings nyid //rje gzhal yas na mkha'i tshad mnyam pa//ka ba yid bzhin bris pa brgya du bstan/rin chen mchog
12b6(dza): sbyin dbang gis yum du ston/kun tu rol pas spyir yang sgrol ba'i dbyings/ba ga maN+Dal g+hA ya sid+d+hi hUM/hUM dbang phyug ma ra bsdus sing dzam pa la/drug gsum zil gnon rnam dag 'joms mdzad cing //mthu rtsal che bas
12b7(dza): khyad par kun las 'phags/don dam gsang sras dbang phyug rgyal ba'i bkal sing nga dzaM b+ha la bi ma la sid+d+hi hrI/hUM khri rkang rin chen 'bar ba'i skur sten pas/yon tan drug stong dpag tshad 'phang dang mnyam/sprin med nyi
13a1(dza): @ma bdun brdegs gsal bas n+yanA/rigs bzhi khrag 'ang sbrul 'brel/rA traM/hU~M dam gyis skyon bral bad+mar ga'i mngag/drin ma dbyibs legs kha dog sa sor gsal/chas dbyings che ba'i
13a2(dza): khyad par rab ston phyir/rigs mchog brjid ldan gsang ba yum gyi mkha'/pad+ma d+hA ra tra~M/hU~M 'jig rten 'od kyi rgyal po nyi zla'i dkyil 'khor ni/thabs mchog brjid ldan 'od zer phyogs bcur 'gyed /stong gsum mun nag kun nas sel
13a3(dza): mdzad pa'i//yes gsal ba'i me long mA ma ki/sur+b+ba maN+Da la hUM/tsan+d+ra a/nyi zla de dkyil gdan stengasu/na mkha'i yi ge hUM phaT ste/rnam gsum tshul gyis sku nyid rdzogs/phyag rgya chen po bzhi dang ldan/oM badz+ra sU ta ya ta ta Ad ma
13a4(dza): ko&haM/bcom ldan dpal mchog rdo rje sems pa ni/sku gsum lhun grub dbu gsum brjid par bstan/mthing ka dkar gsal pad+ma ra ga'i zhal/rgyud drug rtsad gcod yis drug gis phyag/g.yas pa rdo 'khor lo pad+ma 'dzin//
13a5(dza): g.yon pa dril bu rin chen ral gri 'dzin//rnam pa thang mchog dang rab ldan pas/thad rgyan gyis ma lus yongasu rdzogs//spyan drangs mtshad pa rdzogs pa dang /sgrub pa'i don du bsnyen pa 'bul/dbang dang byin rlabs dngos grub
13a6(dza): gsol ba bta ba te gnang thob dmigs byas las/bdag nyid zhe sdang rdo rje las/rigs gnyis lung gis rgyud pa dang /'gro drug gnas nas drangs pa'i phyir/yi ge hU~M d+h+r-ig cho 'phrul bstan/khro bo'i rgyal po 'jigs byed hUM sgra can/hU~M gi

41b2(dza): 'gul//rno gdug lcags kyi pho nya ma/kha na ro bshal yid 'phrog byed//rdo rol pa'i pho nya mo//kha na ro bshal spyang mo gdong //mche ba bdung bzhi zil gyis gnon//dbang gi gzi 'bar pho nya ma//kha na ro ba shal dur khrod 'joms//sbrul nag 'khrig
41b3(dza): ba'i pho nya ma/kha na ro ba shal ri lung 'phyang //mgyogs kyang rlung gi spyodu med//za byed mche ba'i pho nya ma//kha na ro ba shal nga ro sgrogs//srid pa'i lha srin za su za//shu rkan dar gyi pho nya ma//kha na ro ba shal 'jigs pa'i gzugs//'od zer

41b6(dza): gza' skar tsog por 'phen//gdan khri pad+ma'i bar bar nas//dung gi spyang mo gsum ma//spyan gsum dur khrod gnasu gzigs//kha nas ro bshal ru da 'joms//rigs gnyis brgyud pa'i bka' nyan ma//las la bab po pho nya'i tshogs//
41b7(dza): kha lo kha la lo kha//rabang chen bsdus pa'i ha na tra las//spyang mo ro ba shal gyi le'u ste sum cu so cig pa'o///tshol dbang thob rigs 'dzin rna khyor gyis//'khor bsdus pa'i tan tra las//tshar gsum lnga'i le'u
42a1(dza): @#'di gsung so//tshar gsum pa'i rnga brdungs pas//lcang lo can gyi pho brang 'joms//snang mdzad chen mo a la la//ces brjod ka 'khor bsdus pa'i dang por rgyu dang rang bzhin dang //don dang sgom dang grangs dang ni//bsnyen

42a3(dza): por gyur pa dang //ma 'gags par snang bar grags pa'o//sgom pa la yang gsum ste//lta bar sgom pa dang /lha'i sku phyag rgyar sgom pa dang /sprul par sgom pa'o//rnga'i grangs la yang gsum ste/sgrub pa lugs kyi rnga dang //gsang ba yum

42a6(dza): tshe la dbang thob rigs 'dzin rna khyor gyis//dam du bsdus pa'i te na tra las//tshangs dbyangs glu'i le'u 'di gsungs//tshangs pa dbyangs kyi klu drug ni//breng de ba dang dung de ba/shar 'da' ba dang gar 'da' ba//cang ka de'u dang pang ka re'u/

42b7(dza): yang shar du rdo sder mo can/lhor ni ma mo glog 'gyu ma//nub tu rngo dra ba mo//byang du ma mo da ba bzhag/shar lho 'tsham su dgod pa yang //rdo kham rgya chen mo'o//lho nub 'tsham su dgod pa yang //rin chen lam brgya dgong par bya//nub byang 'tsham su

46b6(dza): thugs khar sbas pa'i che ba yis tshe gnas lhun gyis grub pa'i che ba mthar phyin/lhun grub sku la rdzogs pa'i dkyil 'khor gyi yon rdzogs pa'i che ba mthar phyin te brgya do//rtogs pa bdun gyis re dogs kyi dmigs pa gsal ba ni/
46b7(dza): gong ma ltar gang gis rtogs pa ni dz+nyA na tsit+ta dzo la yaM su rtogs pa'o//bud d+he tsit+ta dza las sang su rtogs pa'o//bo d+hi tsit+ta dza las byang chub rtogs pa'o//gong ma ltar ma rtogs te dngo su bzung na ni/rak+Sha tsit+ta dza las srin po rtogs pa'o//5
47a1(dza): @sha tsit+ta dza la gnod sbyin du rtogs pa'o//karma tsit+ta dza las srin por la su zer rtogs pa'o//ma ra ka tsih+Na las dmyal bar rtogs pa'o//dbang chen bsdus pa'i ha ma tra las//man ngag bsdus pa'i le'u ste sum cu rtsa brgyad ba'o//
47a2(dza): tshe la dbang thob rigs 'dzin rna khyor gyis//ro sbyor bdud rtsi bya ba'i phyir//dbang chen bsdus pa'i tan hr-i las//sgrub thabs gsang ba 'di gsungaso//dang por phun sum tshogs ldan nas//yi du 'ong ba'i dgon gnasuka bslang ba dang ni byin gyis brlab pa dang /'tsha/
47a3(dza): la sogs pa rim pa bzhin//maN+Dal 'dom gang gru bzhi ste//bka' dang mthun pa'i chag chag grang ba//thig bzhi gdab pa'i mtha' bskor la/ru dra rnam gsum bsnol te gnas//dbu su zla gam bzhi pa ste//de'i dbu su gru gsum mthing nag gsum pa'o//kha/
47a4(dza): bzhi pa la sbyin bsreg pa'o//mtha' bskor 'bar ba'i 'od kyiso//'thun pa'i ngam rdzas gnas pa'i sar//drug gnyis ldan pa'i ting 'dzin gyis//mtshan mas btab te hUM ces bskor//brtul zhugs chen pos khros pas ni ka gar gyi dbang phyug chen po'i bro//
47a5(dza): brdungs pa tsam gyis las rnams 'grub//phung nyul chen mo ltas mdzad nges//bdag nyid chen po'i skur bsgoms nas//mtshungs pa'i rta mgo ljang ku las//rta'i brtul zhugs chen po ste//hUM ces du mar brjod cing brdung //bdag nyid chen po'i skur
47a6(dza): /bsgoms nas//zhabs las sprul pa'i khyi brgyad kyis//bag chags gnasu sbyangs pa'i phyir//khyi'i brtul zhugs chen po ste//ku ku ra zhes rbad cing brdung //bdag nyid chen po'i bskur bsgoms nas/ka gshog pa rdo gtam pa las//gzung 'dzin
47a7(dza): gnasu ngas bya'i phyir/bya'i brtul zhugs chen po yang //dza zhes brjo cing 'gyilas pa dzor //bongs nyi bzhin po'i slub sgoms nas/mi dmigs sbrul mgo las//pagi brtul lugs chen pos ni//gti mug gnas ngag pa'i phyir/dgug pa'i bahu/
47b1(dza): zhugs hUM dang bcas pa brdung //cha brkyang las kyi spyang mor dmigs//tho bas dbye ba sder mo nyid //ngan song gnas kyi gdul sbyong bya//ting 'dzin phyag rgyar bcas pa dbyeka rdzogs nas maN+Dal nyi du grags//bsten ni dus gsum nyi du rten//nga ni zla
47b2(dza): gam hi du ste//snyigs ma par 'khyams pa bzhi la//rtse mo ma tang ka nyid la'o//sgo zung la ni brtag pa rnams//sgo ni nang rol rdo ste//phyag mtshan bcu drug phrag gnyis lo//yan lag bar du ba su ta//'ong la rak+ta khrag dang bcas//zhing chen bla re nyidas
47b3(dza): rdzi ma skra dang kha spu rnams//mchod pa'i phyir nas gtor ba'o//sa la dbang ba'i ban nga las//phye mar ga tar bcas pa rnams//spyos tsam yang na brdzis pa las//steng nas bcings te rgyas btab ste//sku gsum thos pa shar phyogasu//rak+ta/
47b4(dza): chang dang sbyar ba las ka steng nas rin chen rdos rgyas btab nas//gling gsum pas ni lho phyogs teka/rak+ta 'o mar bsgyur ba la//steng nas rin chen rgyas btab nas//dbang du bsdus pa'i ban+d+ha ru ka rak+ta sbrang rtsi mar la sogs//steng nas pad+mo rgyas bta
47b5(dza): nas gling bzhi rgya gram byang phyog ste//rak+ta chu dang cha mnyam ste//steng nas las kyis rgyas btab pa'o//rdzogs nos ting 'dzin cha su rtsal//'tshams dang bsrung ba go cha'o//phyag rgya bskyed dang 'phro 'du dang //dro tshang dbab dang thugs rje dbab//tshe gan+da
47b6(dza): bstim dang lnga pa'o//yang nas yang du bsnyen pa bya//phrin las skabs dang sbyar nas bcol//dbang dang dam tshig mi bza' ste//zhi ba la sogs dkyil 'khor gyi//'phro 'du rims kyis bsnyen pas sbyang //bskul dang don byas 'dus pa dang //cho ga
47b7(dza): g+ha suM gyis snang mdzad sUka/semasu zhu nas dbyingasu thim//skye med dbyings nas dngos med sku//hUM du gyur nas rtse lnga 'bar//bskul dang ngon byas mi bskyod sku//oM la semasu zhu nas dbyingasu tham//skye mi dbyings nas dngos med sku//swa yas
48a1(dza): @#/rin chen zur brgyang 'gro//bskul dang ngon pas 'byung ldan nyid //sem su zhu nas dbyangs kyi du'o//skye med dbyings nas dngongos med sku//oM las bad+mo gsal ba dang /bskul dbangon byas sna mtha' yas//semasu nu nas dbying sa thim/

48a7(dza): dbul ba ni//rdo kham brgyad chen mo la//phul bas gzi ldan dbyingasu thim//grong pa lho phyogs nyid nas te//rin chen lam brgya chen mo la//dbyangs dang bcas pa lnga ma bzhin//phul bas gzi ldan dbyingasu thim//gnyis ma nub nas snga ma bzhin/
48b1(dza): ma chags pad+ma'i kham brgyal//phul bas gzi ldan b+ha ga la/long ka byang nas rim pa bzhin//don rab lhun 'grub kham rgyal//dbyangs kyis bstod mas mnyes par bya'o//gzi ldan la ni 'phro 'dra'i tshul//sul mangs shar lho mtsham su te//de bzhin lam
48b2(dza): ting 'tshang /byangs dang ldan pas yis dang //rgyu ma yis gyo nub ste//de bzhin lam 'argya nying ba rjong //khyab bas yis kham brkyang pa'o//byungs dang tshig ldan snga ma ling //skal rtsib phyogs bzhi gru ma sa/yong ba'i tshogs la phul ba yang/

48b7(dza): /nyiting la spro//maN+Dal bla gam nyid la ste//zla gam ga'i ldan yum gyi dang //yang na bdag nyid chen po ni//skye ba med pa'i dbyingasu thi ma/maN+Dal yes lnga la bsdu//ngam rdzas gsang ba'i b+ha gar bsdu/yong su bsdu zhing sdu ba rnams//skye ba med pa'i ngang la bzhag
49a1(dza): cung zad skye med bsgoms nas kyang //gong ma bzhin du rim par bsdu//dbang bskur ba dang 'byung ba yang //tshe dang dbang dang dngos grub rnams//bzod pa gsol dang smon lam gdab//gtor ma btang ba gzhung bzhin bya//dbang chen bsdus pa'i

56b2(dza): sku las sdom pa bzhir grags pa//na mkha'i ri mo phyag rgya'i dngos//cir yang 'gyur ba'i bzhugs thabs rngom//zil gyis gnon pa'i gdan mchog rngom//tshang ma chen po'i stangs stabs rngom//gsung las rngom pa bzhir grags pa//hom lngar 'brel ba'i zhal rngom//zla ga ma/
56b3(dza): tsicir yang 'gyur ba'i ljags rngom//skye shi rtsang nas gcod pa'i tshems rngom//gsung sgra 'brug ltar sgrogs pa'i dbyangs rngom//thugs kyi rngom bzhir grags pa//ting 'dzin na mkha'i rgyan gyis skar tshogs lta bu rngom//phrin las rgya mtsho rgyan gyi rlabs lta bu rngom//snyoms

56b5(dza): zhugs rngom//sku'i brjid pa bzhir grags pa/sku tshe yan lag rags pas brjid tshe//grags mang rkyen dang 'tshan pas brjid che//bskur 'os gtso bor mthong bas brjid cha//khya zhugs bla ma yin pas brjid che//gsung gi brjid pa bzhir grags pas//gdangs che stong gsum
56b6(dza): gang bas brjid che//tshig nyung the tshom gcod pas brjid che//don la nyi tsher ston pas brjid che//gsal bas mtha' gnyis spangs pas brjid che//thugs kyi brjid pa bzhir grags pa//thugs rje thugs ngam mthar phyin brjid che//ma rmongs thabs kyis 'dul bar brjid che//

57a7(dza): rigs kyi pho na gtang ba dang //bsnyen pa'i grangs dang rgyas gdab dang //phra dang rig pas mngon sum bsdug lta bas lhan gsal ba'o//de la sbyang bar bya ba yang //sngags dang phyag rgya pho nya dang //chos nyid dag pa'i ngang giso//spel ba'i don dang rang bzhi ni//chos kyis dbyings las/

57b2(dza): skud/bsnyen pa'i sa bom nyi du sgo//'di ni spro ba'i lung du gsungs//bskul dang don byas 'dus pa dang /gzi dang ldan pa'i dpal be'u //sems dpa'i sku dang ldan par bya/rigs kyi pho nya dgye ba ni/ra gar nas rdo pho nya mo//ga shog pa'i lus la rdo'i gdong /lho nas rdo bo ta mo//

58a5(dza): sum tshogs par 'du ba dang //'bor phun sum tshogs pa dang ldan pa dang /kardzas phun sum tshogs pa btsal ba dang //yo byad phun sum tshogs pa bsag pa dang //ting 'dzin phun sum tshogs pas gsal ba dang //phrin las phun sum tshogs pa spyad pa dang //dngos grub phun sum tshon/

58b2(dza): khya bapar dbang gi le'u 'di gsungs//de yang gang dang gang zhe na//ye rgya spyi'i dbang dang /khyang par rim pa'i dbango //ye rgya spyi'i dbang ni rgyud las 'di ska //rgyal thabs spyi blugs theg chen a ti'i dbang //bsku ro tsam gyis thob pa'o//khyad par rim pa'i dbang la yang ngo/

59b1(dza): 'dod ba'i chos lngam spangs pa ga sbyang bas grol sa mya ngan las/phyim sngong bachung ma lus kun/byang rgya chen porang kyis 'phob jug/'dims 'da' ba pa'i bya/ngang du blangs pa'i ngamatshig nig sman 'phan la sogs sbyang ba yang /'das pa'i gdus rnams kyis pa/

60a1(dza): @#/rna khyor gyis//phyi rabs rma khyod rnams kyis don che du ka dbang chen bsdus pa'i han rdzalas ka man ngag sbas pa'i le'u 'di gsungs//pho brang rin chen gzhal yas khang //gang ma dang 'byung bar bcas pa rnams//dbang chen rims kyis bskyed pa ni/

60b1(dza): dbang chen bsdus pa'i tan tra las//yang dag don gyi rnam grangs kyi le'u 'di gsungs//de yang gang dang gang zhe na//ma nor ba rtogs par lta ba yang dag pa dang //ma bgrod bar phyin pa'i sa yang dag pa dang /ma bsgrubs par grub pa'i dngos grub yang dag pa dang /gnyisu/
60b2(dza): med pa'i spyod pa yang dag pa dang //bsrung du med pa'i dam tshig yang dag pa dang /'bad ba med pa'i brtson 'grus yang dag pa dang /khris pa med pa'i dkyil 'khor yang dag pa dang /sgom du med pa'i lha yang dag pa dang /bskur ba med pa'i dbang yang dag pa dang /ma song bar byin/

60b7(dza): 'gruso//zhes 'byung bri ba med pa'i dkyil 'khor yang dag pa nig rgyud las/'byung ba yum gyis gzhal yas nas//snang srid phyag rgya'i dkyil 'khor yin//bri ba bya ba'i ming yang med//ces 'byung /sbom du med pa'i lha yang dag pa ni//rgyud las/bdag nyid chen por
61a1(dza): don rtogs la/sangs rgyas kun gyi nying gcan//bsgoms pas gzhan nas mi D+ho //zhes 'byung /bskur ba med pa'i dbang yang dag pa ni/t+yA las/dbyings dang mes don rtogs na/thabs dang shes rab bang chen yin/rdo dril bu rigs pas bzung /
61a2(dza): zhes 'byung /ma song bar phyin pa'i gnas yang dag pa ni/rgyud las/khams gsum yum gyi b+ha ga las//gang ltar song yang gnas kyis mchog zhes 'byung /brjod med pa'i tshogs yang dag pa ni/rgyu las/mtshan ma ming du btags pa yang /mtshan ma med pa'i chos yin pas/
61a3(dza): tshig tu brjod pa'i tha snyad bral zhes 'byung //don de lta bas na/rna khyor gyis dbang phyug chen pos//don de ltar yid ches nas/rtogs pa'i klong du gyur nas bsgoms pa ni//bud d+ha tsit+ta dza la yino//dz+nyA na tsit+ta dza la yi no//bo d+hi tsit+ta dza la yi no//don 'di la yid ma ches shing ///
61a4(dza): yodang ma yin zhe 'am/'di lta bu'i lta ba gtan la mi phebs zhes skur pa 'debs shing rtogs pa klong du ma gyur na ni/karma tsitu dza la yi no//rag sha tsit+ta dza la yi no//dbang chen bsdus pa'i ta na tra las//yang dag don rnams grangs kyi le'u ste lnga bcu drug pa'o//

61a6(dza): lhan sgom//'bar ba'i rta mgo rma du byung ba ni//lang ka la sogs he ru gsum du snang //sku gsung thugs las ma g.yos lha ni sgomaka 'bar ba'i ral pa rma byung ba ni//dbyings las ma g.yos chos sku 'bar ba las//khro bo mu nau dza'i cod pan bye ba 'bum//sbrul

61b3(dza): gsung rab mang du 'chang //sde snod rgyud la rmongs pa mi mngar ba'i//le'u rman chags pas 'gro ba sgrol ba bsgom//'bar ba'i ag chom rma du byung ba ni//chos sku mtshan ma med pa'i ngo bo las//gzugs sku 'ja' tsho na lta bur sprul par sgom//'bar ba'i

61b5(dza): kha Ta kha/zong rlon khrag dang rdo rje lu gu brgyud //rdo lcags kyu dang ni rje'u chung 'dzin//sdigs mdzub rtsel me 'bar rdo kha T+wAM sgrol//rdo rje rtse gcig rtse gsum rtse lnga rtse dgu 'bar ba gsor zhing brdeg/so so'i phyag mtshan ngong su gsal ba yang //snang tsam nyid na/

62a1(dza): gzhan gyi dngos grub bdag gir bya ba dang ka mnyam pa chen po'i don la dgod pa'i phyir//dbyings dang yis gnyisu sbyor bar sgomaka 'bar ba'i gzhal yas rma du byung ba ni ka sna tshogs mang po rgya mdang dkram pa yang //ma mkha' mtha' dang dbyer med stong pas na//

62b1(dza): lam bu rtsol ba ni ka goms pa'i mthu med 'phags lam ring na yang //lha ba rgya mtsho bzhin du blta bar bya//byin brlabs chen pos sang+yas khyad par can//thams cad lha yin pas ma mngon sum pas//rig pa dang ni goms par bcas pa yin/sa dpa sa'i rim

62b5(dza): spyod yul ma ma yin te//dbye ba med pa'i ngang du rdzogs sangs rgyas//lta ba rgya mtsho'i gting ltar ye nas ngag/de bas lta ba rgya mtsho bzhin du lha//gu h+ya sa ma ya'i thugs ka nas//skal ldan 'ga'i rkyen du gsungs pa 'di ka snod med phal gyi spyod yul

62b7(dza): d+han tra las//lha ba rgya mtsho'i le'u ste lnga bcu brgyad pa'o///rme la dadang thob rigs 'dzin rna khyod gyis//dbang chen bsdus pa'i tan tra las//chos rnams kun gyis gzhi rtsa ni//sems dang kum gzhing brta pa'i phyir//gtan la thab pani
63a1(dza): /le'u 'di gsungs//de yang chos rnams rtsa ba kun gzhi nyid la 'dus/kun gzhi gnas 'gyur yis lngar snang ba//de lta ba'i don la mnyam par gzhag pas na//kun gzhi gnas 'gyur ba yang de nyid yin//yaMs lnga nyid de na tshangs pas na//yes/
